#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
        
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            
            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }
        
        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
    printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
    printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Initialize bodies
    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
    randomizeBodies(bodies);
    
    // Run simulation
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies);
        integrateBodies(bodies);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
        
        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

The force calculation is quadratic and uses double precision. I’ll give each CUDA thread a body, tile source positions in shared memory, and keep state on the GPU between steps. Separate position buffers will let me combine force calculation and integration while preserving the original update order.

#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
        
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            
            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }
        
        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
    printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
    printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Initialize bodies
    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
    randomizeBodies(bodies);
    
    // Run simulation
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies);
        integrateBodies(bodies);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
        
        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+// Each thread owns one target body. Source positions are loaded cooperatively
+// into shared memory and reused by all targets in the block.
+template <int BlockSize>
+__global__ void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positions,
+                              double* __restrict__ nextPositions,
+                              double* __restrict__ velocities,
+                              size_t n, size_t stride) {
+    __shared__ double tile[3][BlockSize];
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * BlockSize + lane;
+    const bool active = i < n;
+    const double px = active ? positions[i] : 0.0;
+    const double py = active ? positions[stride + i] : 0.0;
+    const double pz = active ? positions[2 * stride + i] : 0.0;
+    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < n; base += BlockSize) {
+        const size_t j = base + lane;
+        tile[0][lane] = j < n ? positions[j] : 0.0;
+        tile[1][lane] = j < n ? positions[stride + j] : 0.0;
+        tile[2][lane] = j < n ? positions[2 * stride + j] : 0.0;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(n - base < BlockSize ? n - base : BlockSize);
+        if (active) {
+            // Preserve increasing source-index order; no parallel force reduction
+            // or reduced-precision/approximate reciprocal square root is used.
+            #pragma unroll 4
+            for (int k = 0; k < count; ++k) {
+                const double dx = tile[0][k] - px;
+                const double dy = tile[1][k] - py;
+                const double dz = tile[2][k] - pz;
+                const double distSqr = __fma_rn(dz, dz, __fma_rn(dx, dx, dy * dy)) + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+                fx = __fma_rn(dx, invDist3, fx);
+                fy = __fma_rn(dy, invDist3, fy);
+                fz = __fma_rn(dz, invDist3, fz);
+            }
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        // Inactive threads in a partial block must also reach both barriers.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-}

+        const double vy = __dadd_rn(velocities[stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fy));
+        const double vz = __dadd_rn(velocities[2 * stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fz));
+        velocities[i] = vx;
+        velocities[stride + i] = vy;
+        velocities[2 * stride + i] = vz;
+        // A separate output buffer keeps every force calculation on the same
+        // position snapshot, even though integration is fused into this kernel.
+        nextPositions[i] = __fma_rn(vx, DT, px);
+        nextPositions[stride + i] = __fma_rn(vy, DT, py);
+        nextPositions[2 * stride + i] = __fma_rn(vz, DT, pz);
     }
 }
 
+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies)
+        : n_(bodies.size()), stride_((n_ + 31) / 32 * 32), state_(6 * stride_) {
+        // CUDA is required even for an empty simulation; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "initializing the CUDA runtime");
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            state_[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            state_[stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            state_[2 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            state_[3 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            state_[4 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            state_[5 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&storage_), 9 * stride_ * sizeof(double)),
+                  "allocating body state");
+        positions_ = storage_;
+        nextPositions_ = storage_ + 3 * stride_;
+        velocities_ = storage_ + 6 * stride_;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_, state_.data(), 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "uploading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, state_.data() + 3 * stride_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "uploading velocities");
+    }
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (storage_) checkCuda(cudaFree(storage_), "freeing body state");
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    void run(int steps) {
+        if (n_ == 0 || steps <= 0) return;
+        for (int step = 0; step < steps; ++step) {
+            // Smaller blocks expose more independent bodies for small problems.
+            if (n_ < 4096) {
+                launch<32>();
+            } else if (n_ < 16384) {
+                launch<64>();
+            } else {
+                launch<128>();
+            }
+            std::swap(positions_, nextPositions_);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the simulation kernels");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "running the simulation");
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data(), positions_, 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data() + 3 * stride_, velocities_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "downloading velocities");
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(state_[i], state_[stride_ + i], state_[2 * stride_ + i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(state_[3 * stride_ + i], state_[4 * stride_ + i],
+                                 state_[5 * stride_ + i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    template <int BlockSize>
+    void launch() {
+        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>((n_ + BlockSize - 1) / BlockSize);
+        advanceBodies<BlockSize><<<blocks, BlockSize>>>(positions_, nextPositions_, velocities_, n_, stride_);
+    }
+
+    const size_t n_, stride_;
+    std::vector<double> state_;
+    double* storage_ = nullptr;
+    double* positions_ = nullptr;
+    double* nextPositions_ = nullptr;
+    double* velocities_ = nullptr;
+};
+

     double energy = 0.0;
     const size_t n = bodies.size();
@@ -158,17 +276,16 @@

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+// Each thread owns one target body. Source positions are loaded cooperatively
+// into shared memory and reused by all targets in the block.
+template <int BlockSize>
+__global__ void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positions,
+                              double* __restrict__ nextPositions,
+                              double* __restrict__ velocities,
+                              size_t n, size_t stride) {
+    __shared__ double tile[3][BlockSize];
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * BlockSize + lane;
+    const bool active = i < n;
+    const double px = active ? positions[i] : 0.0;
+    const double py = active ? positions[stride + i] : 0.0;
+    const double pz = active ? positions[2 * stride + i] : 0.0;
+    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < n; base += BlockSize) {
+        const size_t j = base + lane;
+        tile[0][lane] = j < n ? positions[j] : 0.0;
+        tile[1][lane] = j < n ? positions[stride + j] : 0.0;
+        tile[2][lane] = j < n ? positions[2 * stride + j] : 0.0;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(n - base < BlockSize ? n - base : BlockSize);
+        if (active) {
+            // Preserve increasing source-index order; no parallel force reduction
+            // or reduced-precision/approximate reciprocal square root is used.
+            #pragma unroll 4
+            for (int k = 0; k < count; ++k) {
+                const double dx = tile[0][k] - px;
+                const double dy = tile[1][k] - py;
+                const double dz = tile[2][k] - pz;
+                const double distSqr = __fma_rn(dz, dz, __fma_rn(dx, dx, dy * dy)) + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+                fx = __fma_rn(dx, invDist3, fx);
+                fy = __fma_rn(dy, invDist3, fy);
+                fz = __fma_rn(dz, invDist3, fz);
+            }
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        // Inactive threads in a partial block must also reach both barriers.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-}

-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+    if (active) {
+        const double vx = __dadd_rn(velocities[i], __dmul_rn(DT, fx));
+        const double vy = __dadd_rn(velocities[stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fy));
+        const double vz = __dadd_rn(velocities[2 * stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fz));
+        velocities[i] = vx;
+        velocities[stride + i] = vy;
+        velocities[2 * stride + i] = vz;
+        // A separate output buffer keeps every force calculation on the same
+        // position snapshot, even though integration is fused into this kernel.
+        nextPositions[i] = __fma_rn(vx, DT, px);
+        nextPositions[stride + i] = __fma_rn(vy, DT, py);
+        nextPositions[2 * stride + i] = __fma_rn(vz, DT, pz);
     }
 }

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+// Each thread owns one target body. Source positions are loaded cooperatively
+// into shared memory and reused by all targets in the block.
+template <int BlockSize>
+__global__ void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positions,
+                              double* __restrict__ nextPositions,
+                              double* __restrict__ velocities,
+                              size_t n, size_t stride) {
+    __shared__ double tile[3][BlockSize];
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * BlockSize + lane;
+    const bool active = i < n;
+    const double px = active ? positions[i] : 0.0;
+    const double py = active ? positions[stride + i] : 0.0;
+    const double pz = active ? positions[2 * stride + i] : 0.0;
+    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < n; base += BlockSize) {
+        const size_t j = base + lane;
+        tile[0][lane] = j < n ? positions[j] : 0.0;
+        tile[1][lane] = j < n ? positions[stride + j] : 0.0;
+        tile[2][lane] = j < n ? positions[2 * stride + j] : 0.0;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(n - base < BlockSize ? n - base : BlockSize);
+        if (active) {
+            // Preserve increasing source-index order; no parallel force reduction
+            // or reduced-precision/approximate reciprocal square root is used.
+            #pragma unroll 4
+            for (int k = 0; k < count; ++k) {
+                const double dx = tile[0][k] - px;
+                const double dy = tile[1][k] - py;
+                const double dz = tile[2][k] - pz;
+                const double distSqr = __fma_rn(dz, dz, __fma_rn(dx, dx, dy * dy)) + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+                fx = __fma_rn(dx, invDist3, fx);
+                fy = __fma_rn(dy, invDist3, fy);
+                fz = __fma_rn(dz, invDist3, fz);
+            }
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        // Inactive threads in a partial block must also reach both barriers.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-}
 
-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {

-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+    if (active) {
+        const double vx = __dadd_rn(velocities[i], __dmul_rn(DT, fx));
+        const double vy = __dadd_rn(velocities[stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fy));
+        const double vz = __dadd_rn(velocities[2 * stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fz));
+        velocities[i] = vx;
+        velocities[stride + i] = vy;
+        velocities[2 * stride + i] = vz;
+        // A separate output buffer keeps every force calculation on the same
+        // position snapshot, even though integration is fused into this kernel.
+        nextPositions[i] = __fma_rn(vx, DT, px);
+        nextPositions[stride + i] = __fma_rn(vy, DT, py);
+        nextPositions[2 * stride + i] = __fma_rn(vz, DT, pz);
     }
 }
 
+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies)
+        : n_(bodies.size()), stride_((n_ + 31) / 32 * 32), state_(6 * stride_) {
+        // CUDA is required even for an empty simulation; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "initializing the CUDA runtime");
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            state_[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            state_[stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            state_[2 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            state_[3 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            state_[4 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            state_[5 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&storage_), 9 * stride_ * sizeof(double)),
+                  "allocating body state");
+        positions_ = storage_;
+        nextPositions_ = storage_ + 3 * stride_;
+        velocities_ = storage_ + 6 * stride_;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_, state_.data(), 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "uploading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, state_.data() + 3 * stride_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "uploading velocities");
+    }
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (storage_) checkCuda(cudaFree(storage_), "freeing body state");
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    void run(int steps) {
+        if (n_ == 0 || steps <= 0) return;
+        for (int step = 0; step < steps; ++step) {
+            // Smaller blocks expose more independent bodies for small problems.
+            if (n_ < 4096) {
+                launch<32>();
+            } else if (n_ < 16384) {
+                launch<64>();
+            } else {
+                launch<128>();
+            }
+            std::swap(positions_, nextPositions_);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the simulation kernels");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "running the simulation");
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data(), positions_, 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data() + 3 * stride_, velocities_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "downloading velocities");
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(state_[i], state_[stride_ + i], state_[2 * stride_ + i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(state_[3 * stride_ + i], state_[4 * stride_ + i],
+                                 state_[5 * stride_ + i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    template <int BlockSize>
+    void launch() {
+        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>((n_ + BlockSize - 1) / BlockSize);
+        advanceBodies<BlockSize><<<blocks, BlockSize>>>(positions_, nextPositions_, velocities_, n_, stride_);
+    }
+
+    const size_t n_, stride_;
+    std::vector<double> state_;
+    double* storage_ = nullptr;
+    double* positions_ = nullptr;
+    double* nextPositions_ = nullptr;
+    double* velocities_ = nullptr;
+};

+        velocities[2 * stride + i] = vz;
+        // A separate output buffer keeps every force calculation on the same
+        // position snapshot, even though integration is fused into this kernel.
+        nextPositions[i] = __fma_rn(vx, DT, px);
+        nextPositions[stride + i] = __fma_rn(vy, DT, py);
+        nextPositions[2 * stride + i] = __fma_rn(vz, DT, pz);
     }
 }
 
+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies)
+        : n_(bodies.size()), stride_((n_ + 31) / 32 * 32), state_(6 * stride_) {
+        // CUDA is required even for an empty simulation; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "initializing the CUDA runtime");
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            state_[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            state_[stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            state_[2 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            state_[3 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            state_[4 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            state_[5 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&storage_), 9 * stride_ * sizeof(double)),
+                  "allocating body state");
+        positions_ = storage_;
+        nextPositions_ = storage_ + 3 * stride_;
+        velocities_ = storage_ + 6 * stride_;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_, state_.data(), 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "uploading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, state_.data() + 3 * stride_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "uploading velocities");
+    }
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (storage_) checkCuda(cudaFree(storage_), "freeing body state");
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    void run(int steps) {
+        if (n_ == 0 || steps <= 0) return;
+        for (int step = 0; step < steps; ++step) {
+            // Smaller blocks expose more independent bodies for small problems.
+            if (n_ < 4096) {
+                launch<32>();
+            } else if (n_ < 16384) {
+                launch<64>();
+            } else {
+                launch<128>();
+            }
+            std::swap(positions_, nextPositions_);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the simulation kernels");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "running the simulation");
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data(), positions_, 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data() + 3 * stride_, velocities_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "downloading velocities");
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(state_[i], state_[stride_ + i], state_[2 * stride_ + i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(state_[3 * stride_ + i], state_[4 * stride_ + i],
+                                 state_[5 * stride_ + i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    template <int BlockSize>
+    void launch() {
+        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>((n_ + BlockSize - 1) / BlockSize);
+        advanceBodies<BlockSize><<<blocks, BlockSize>>>(positions_, nextPositions_, velocities_, n_, stride_);
+    }
+
+    const size_t n_, stride_;
+    std::vector<double> state_;
+    double* storage_ = nullptr;
+    double* positions_ = nullptr;
+    double* nextPositions_ = nullptr;
+    double* velocities_ = nullptr;
+};

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }

+// Each thread owns one target body. Source positions are loaded cooperatively
+// into shared memory and reused by all targets in the block.
+template <int BlockSize>
+__global__ void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positions,
+                              double* __restrict__ nextPositions,
+                              double* __restrict__ velocities,
+                              size_t n, size_t stride) {
+    __shared__ double tile[3][BlockSize];
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * BlockSize + lane;
+    const bool active = i < n;
+    const double px = active ? positions[i] : 0.0;
+    const double py = active ? positions[stride + i] : 0.0;
+    const double pz = active ? positions[2 * stride + i] : 0.0;
+    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < n; base += BlockSize) {
+        const size_t j = base + lane;
+        tile[0][lane] = j < n ? positions[j] : 0.0;
+        tile[1][lane] = j < n ? positions[stride + j] : 0.0;
+        tile[2][lane] = j < n ? positions[2 * stride + j] : 0.0;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(n - base < BlockSize ? n - base : BlockSize);
+        if (active) {
+            // Preserve increasing source-index order; no parallel force reduction
+            // or reduced-precision/approximate reciprocal square root is used.
+            #pragma unroll 4
+            for (int k = 0; k < count; ++k) {
+                const double dx = tile[0][k] - px;
+                const double dy = tile[1][k] - py;
+                const double dz = tile[2][k] - pz;
+                const double distSqr = __fma_rn(dz, dz, __fma_rn(dx, dx, dy * dy)) + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+                fx = __fma_rn(dx, invDist3, fx);
+                fy = __fma_rn(dy, invDist3, fy);
+                fz = __fma_rn(dz, invDist3, fz);
+            }
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        // Inactive threads in a partial block must also reach both barriers.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-}

+        const double vx = __dadd_rn(velocities[i], __dmul_rn(DT, fx));
+        const double vy = __dadd_rn(velocities[stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fy));
+        const double vz = __dadd_rn(velocities[2 * stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fz));
+        velocities[i] = vx;
+        velocities[stride + i] = vy;
+        velocities[2 * stride + i] = vz;
+        // A separate output buffer keeps every force calculation on the same
+        // position snapshot, even though integration is fused into this kernel.
+        nextPositions[i] = __fma_rn(vx, DT, px);
+        nextPositions[stride + i] = __fma_rn(vy, DT, py);
+        nextPositions[2 * stride + i] = __fma_rn(vz, DT, pz);
     }

+            for (int k = 0; k < count; ++k) {
+                const double dx = tile[0][k] - px;
+                const double dy = tile[1][k] - py;
+                const double dz = tile[2][k] - pz;
+                const double distSqr = __fma_rn(dz, dz, __fma_rn(dx, dx, dy * dy)) + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+                fx = __fma_rn(dx, invDist3, fx);
+                fy = __fma_rn(dy, invDist3, fy);
+                fz = __fma_rn(dz, invDist3, fz);
+            }
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        // Inactive threads in a partial block must also reach both barriers.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-}
 
-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+    if (active) {
+        const double vx = __dadd_rn(velocities[i], __dmul_rn(DT, fx));
+        const double vy = __dadd_rn(velocities[stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fy));
+        const double vz = __dadd_rn(velocities[2 * stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fz));
+        velocities[i] = vx;
+        velocities[stride + i] = vy;
+        velocities[2 * stride + i] = vz;

+        // position snapshot, even though integration is fused into this kernel.
+        nextPositions[i] = __fma_rn(vx, DT, px);
+        nextPositions[stride + i] = __fma_rn(vy, DT, py);
+        nextPositions[2 * stride + i] = __fma_rn(vz, DT, pz);
     }
 }
 
+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies)
+        : n_(bodies.size()), stride_((n_ + 31) / 32 * 32), state_(6 * stride_) {
+        // CUDA is required even for an empty simulation; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "initializing the CUDA runtime");
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            state_[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            state_[stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            state_[2 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            state_[3 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            state_[4 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            state_[5 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&storage_), 9 * stride_ * sizeof(double)),
+                  "allocating body state");
+        positions_ = storage_;
+        nextPositions_ = storage_ + 3 * stride_;
+        velocities_ = storage_ + 6 * stride_;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_, state_.data(), 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "uploading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, state_.data() + 3 * stride_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "uploading velocities");
+    }
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (storage_) checkCuda(cudaFree(storage_), "freeing body state");
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    void run(int steps) {
+        if (n_ == 0 || steps <= 0) return;
+        for (int step = 0; step < steps; ++step) {
+            // Smaller blocks expose more independent bodies for small problems.
+            if (n_ < 4096) {
+                launch<32>();
+            } else if (n_ < 16384) {
+                launch<64>();
+            } else {
+                launch<128>();
+            }
+            std::swap(positions_, nextPositions_);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the simulation kernels");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "running the simulation");
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data(), positions_, 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data() + 3 * stride_, velocities_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "downloading velocities");
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(state_[i], state_[stride_ + i], state_[2 * stride_ + i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(state_[3 * stride_ + i], state_[4 * stride_ + i],
+                                 state_[5 * stride_ + i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    template <int BlockSize>
+    void launch() {
+        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>((n_ + BlockSize - 1) / BlockSize);
+        advanceBodies<BlockSize><<<blocks, BlockSize>>>(positions_, nextPositions_, velocities_, n_, stride_);
+    }
+
+    const size_t n_, stride_;
+    std::vector<double> state_;
+    double* storage_ = nullptr;
+    double* positions_ = nullptr;
+    double* nextPositions_ = nullptr;
+    double* velocities_ = nullptr;
+};
+

     double energy = 0.0;
     const size_t n = bodies.size();
@@ -158,17 +276,16 @@

     std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
     randomizeBodies(bodies);

     // Run simulation

-    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
-        computeForces(bodies);
-        integrateBodies(bodies);
-    }
+    simulation.run(numSteps);
     
     auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
     auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
+    simulation.download(bodies);
     
     printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+// Each thread owns one target body. Source positions are loaded cooperatively
+// into shared memory and reused by all targets in the block.
+template <int BlockSize>
+__global__ void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positions,
+                              double* __restrict__ nextPositions,
+                              double* __restrict__ velocities,
+                              size_t n, size_t stride) {
+    __shared__ double tile[3][BlockSize];
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * BlockSize + lane;
+    const bool active = i < n;
+    const double px = active ? positions[i] : 0.0;
+    const double py = active ? positions[stride + i] : 0.0;
+    const double pz = active ? positions[2 * stride + i] : 0.0;
+    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < n; base += BlockSize) {
+        const size_t j = base + lane;
+        tile[0][lane] = j < n ? positions[j] : 0.0;
+        tile[1][lane] = j < n ? positions[stride + j] : 0.0;
+        tile[2][lane] = j < n ? positions[2 * stride + j] : 0.0;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(n - base < BlockSize ? n - base : BlockSize);
+        if (active) {
+            // Preserve increasing source-index order; no parallel force reduction
+            // or reduced-precision/approximate reciprocal square root is used.
+            #pragma unroll 4
+            for (int k = 0; k < count; ++k) {
+                const double dx = tile[0][k] - px;
+                const double dy = tile[1][k] - py;
+                const double dz = tile[2][k] - pz;
+                const double distSqr = __fma_rn(dz, dz, __fma_rn(dx, dx, dy * dy)) + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+                fx = __fma_rn(dx, invDist3, fx);
+                fy = __fma_rn(dy, invDist3, fy);
+                fz = __fma_rn(dz, invDist3, fz);
+            }
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        // Inactive threads in a partial block must also reach both barriers.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-}

+        velocities[stride + i] = vy;
+        velocities[2 * stride + i] = vz;
+        // A separate output buffer keeps every force calculation on the same
+        // position snapshot, even though integration is fused into this kernel.
+        nextPositions[i] = __fma_rn(vx, DT, px);
+        nextPositions[stride + i] = __fma_rn(vy, DT, py);
+        nextPositions[2 * stride + i] = __fma_rn(vz, DT, pz);
     }

#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

#include <cuda_runtime.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
    }
}

// Each thread owns one target body. Source positions are loaded cooperatively
// into shared memory and reused by all targets in the block.
template <int BlockSize>
__global__ void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positions,
                              double* __restrict__ nextPositions,
                              double* __restrict__ velocities,
                              size_t n, size_t stride) {
    __shared__ double tile[3][BlockSize];
    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x;
    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * BlockSize + lane;
    const bool active = i < n;
    const double px = active ? positions[i] : 0.0;
    const double py = active ? positions[stride + i] : 0.0;
    const double pz = active ? positions[2 * stride + i] : 0.0;
    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;

    for (size_t base = 0; base < n; base += BlockSize) {
        const size_t j = base + lane;
        tile[0][lane] = j < n ? positions[j] : 0.0;
        tile[1][lane] = j < n ? positions[stride + j] : 0.0;
        tile[2][lane] = j < n ? positions[2 * stride + j] : 0.0;
        __syncthreads();

        const int count = static_cast<int>(n - base < BlockSize ? n - base : BlockSize);
        if (active) {
            // Preserve increasing source-index order; no parallel force reduction
            // or reduced-precision/approximate reciprocal square root is used.
            #pragma unroll 4
            for (int k = 0; k < count; ++k) {
                const double dx = tile[0][k] - px;
                const double dy = tile[1][k] - py;
                const double dz = tile[2][k] - pz;
                const double distSqr = __fma_rn(dz, dz, __fma_rn(dx, dx, dy * dy)) + SOFTENING;
                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
                fx = __fma_rn(dx, invDist3, fx);
                fy = __fma_rn(dy, invDist3, fy);
                fz = __fma_rn(dz, invDist3, fz);
            }
        }
        // Inactive threads in a partial block must also reach both barriers.
        __syncthreads();
    }

    if (active) {
        const double vx = __dadd_rn(velocities[i], __dmul_rn(DT, fx));
        const double vy = __dadd_rn(velocities[stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fy));
        const double vz = __dadd_rn(velocities[2 * stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fz));
        velocities[i] = vx;
        velocities[stride + i] = vy;
        velocities[2 * stride + i] = vz;
        // A separate output buffer keeps every force calculation on the same
        // position snapshot, even though integration is fused into this kernel.
        nextPositions[i] = __fma_rn(vx, DT, px);
        nextPositions[stride + i] = __fma_rn(vy, DT, py);
        nextPositions[2 * stride + i] = __fma_rn(vz, DT, pz);
    }
}

class CudaSimulation {
public:
    explicit CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies)
        : n_(bodies.size()), stride_((n_ + 31) / 32 * 32), state_(6 * stride_) {
        // CUDA is required even for an empty simulation; there is no CPU fallback.
        checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "initializing the CUDA runtime");
        if (n_ == 0) return;

        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
            state_[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
            state_[stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
            state_[2 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
            state_[3 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
            state_[4 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
            state_[5 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
        }

        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&storage_), 9 * stride_ * sizeof(double)),
                  "allocating body state");
        positions_ = storage_;
        nextPositions_ = storage_ + 3 * stride_;
        velocities_ = storage_ + 6 * stride_;
        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_, state_.data(), 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "uploading positions");
        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, state_.data() + 3 * stride_,
                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
                  "uploading velocities");
    }

    ~CudaSimulation() {
        if (storage_) checkCuda(cudaFree(storage_), "freeing body state");
    }

    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;

    void run(int steps) {
        if (n_ == 0 || steps <= 0) return;
        for (int step = 0; step < steps; ++step) {
            // Smaller blocks expose more independent bodies for small problems.
            if (n_ < 4096) {
                launch<32>();
            } else if (n_ < 16384) {
                launch<64>();
            } else {
                launch<128>();
            }
            std::swap(positions_, nextPositions_);
        }
        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the simulation kernels");
        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "running the simulation");
    }

    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
        if (n_ == 0) return;
        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data(), positions_, 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading positions");
        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data() + 3 * stride_, velocities_,
                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
                  "downloading velocities");
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(state_[i], state_[stride_ + i], state_[2 * stride_ + i]);
            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(state_[3 * stride_ + i], state_[4 * stride_ + i],
                                 state_[5 * stride_ + i]);
        }
    }

private:
    template <int BlockSize>
    void launch() {
        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>((n_ + BlockSize - 1) / BlockSize);
        advanceBodies<BlockSize><<<blocks, BlockSize>>>(positions_, nextPositions_, velocities_, n_, stride_);
    }

    const size_t n_, stride_;
    std::vector<double> state_;
    double* storage_ = nullptr;
    double* positions_ = nullptr;
    double* nextPositions_ = nullptr;
    double* velocities_ = nullptr;
};

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }

+        nextPositions[i] = __fma_rn(vx, DT, px);
+        nextPositions[stride + i] = __fma_rn(vy, DT, py);
+        nextPositions[2 * stride + i] = __fma_rn(vz, DT, pz);
     }
 }
 
+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies)
+        : n_(bodies.size()), stride_((n_ + 31) / 32 * 32), state_(6 * stride_) {
+        // CUDA is required even for an empty simulation; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "initializing the CUDA runtime");
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            state_[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            state_[stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            state_[2 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            state_[3 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            state_[4 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            state_[5 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&storage_), 9 * stride_ * sizeof(double)),
+                  "allocating body state");
+        positions_ = storage_;
+        nextPositions_ = storage_ + 3 * stride_;
+        velocities_ = storage_ + 6 * stride_;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_, state_.data(), 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "uploading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, state_.data() + 3 * stride_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "uploading velocities");
+    }
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (storage_) checkCuda(cudaFree(storage_), "freeing body state");
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    void run(int steps) {
+        if (n_ == 0 || steps <= 0) return;
+        for (int step = 0; step < steps; ++step) {
+            // Smaller blocks expose more independent bodies for small problems.
+            if (n_ < 4096) {
+                launch<32>();
+            } else if (n_ < 16384) {
+                launch<64>();
+            } else {
+                launch<128>();
+            }
+            std::swap(positions_, nextPositions_);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the simulation kernels");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "running the simulation");
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data(), positions_, 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data() + 3 * stride_, velocities_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "downloading velocities");
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(state_[i], state_[stride_ + i], state_[2 * stride_ + i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(state_[3 * stride_ + i], state_[4 * stride_ + i],
+                                 state_[5 * stride_ + i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    template <int BlockSize>
+    void launch() {
+        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>((n_ + BlockSize - 1) / BlockSize);
+        advanceBodies<BlockSize><<<blocks, BlockSize>>>(positions_, nextPositions_, velocities_, n_, stride_);
+    }
+
+    const size_t n_, stride_;
+    std::vector<double> state_;
+    double* storage_ = nullptr;
+    double* positions_ = nullptr;
+    double* nextPositions_ = nullptr;
+    double* velocities_ = nullptr;
+};

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }

+
+// Each thread owns one target body. Source positions are loaded cooperatively
+// into shared memory and reused by all targets in the block.
+template <int BlockSize>
+__global__ void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positions,
+                              double* __restrict__ nextPositions,
+                              double* __restrict__ velocities,
+                              size_t n, size_t stride) {
+    __shared__ double tile[3][BlockSize];
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * BlockSize + lane;
+    const bool active = i < n;
+    const double px = active ? positions[i] : 0.0;
+    const double py = active ? positions[stride + i] : 0.0;
+    const double pz = active ? positions[2 * stride + i] : 0.0;
+    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < n; base += BlockSize) {
+        const size_t j = base + lane;
+        tile[0][lane] = j < n ? positions[j] : 0.0;
+        tile[1][lane] = j < n ? positions[stride + j] : 0.0;
+        tile[2][lane] = j < n ? positions[2 * stride + j] : 0.0;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(n - base < BlockSize ? n - base : BlockSize);
+        if (active) {
+            // Preserve increasing source-index order; no parallel force reduction
+            // or reduced-precision/approximate reciprocal square root is used.
+            #pragma unroll 4
+            for (int k = 0; k < count; ++k) {
+                const double dx = tile[0][k] - px;
+                const double dy = tile[1][k] - py;
+                const double dz = tile[2][k] - pz;
+                const double distSqr = __fma_rn(dz, dz, __fma_rn(dx, dx, dy * dy)) + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+                fx = __fma_rn(dx, invDist3, fx);
+                fy = __fma_rn(dy, invDist3, fy);
+                fz = __fma_rn(dz, invDist3, fz);
+            }
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        // Inactive threads in a partial block must also reach both barriers.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-}

-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;

+        const double vx = __dadd_rn(velocities[i], __dmul_rn(DT, fx));
+        const double vy = __dadd_rn(velocities[stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fy));
+        const double vz = __dadd_rn(velocities[2 * stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fz));
+        velocities[i] = vx;
+        velocities[stride + i] = vy;
+        velocities[2 * stride + i] = vz;
+        // A separate output buffer keeps every force calculation on the same
+        // position snapshot, even though integration is fused into this kernel.
+        nextPositions[i] = __fma_rn(vx, DT, px);
+        nextPositions[stride + i] = __fma_rn(vy, DT, py);
+        nextPositions[2 * stride + i] = __fma_rn(vz, DT, pz);

+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, int steps)
+        : n_(bodies.size()), stride_((n_ + 31) / 32 * 32), state_(6 * stride_) {
+        // CUDA is required even for an empty simulation; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "initializing the CUDA runtime");
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            state_[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            state_[stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            state_[2 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            state_[3 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            state_[4 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            state_[5 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&storage_), 9 * stride_ * sizeof(double)),
+                  "allocating body state");
+        positions_ = storage_;
+        nextPositions_ = storage_ + 3 * stride_;
+        velocities_ = storage_ + 6 * stride_;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_, state_.data(), 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "uploading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, state_.data() + 3 * stride_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "uploading velocities");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "creating the simulation stream");
+
+        if (steps >= 4) {
+            // An even batch returns to the same position buffer, so this graph
+            // can be replayed without updating any device pointers or nodes.
+            graphSteps_ = steps < 32 ? steps / 2 * 2 : 32;
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream_, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                      "capturing the simulation graph");
+            for (int step = 0; step < graphSteps_; ++step) advance();
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "capturing simulation kernels");
+            cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream_, &graph), "finishing the simulation graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                      "instantiating the simulation graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "freeing the captured graph");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (graph_) checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(graph_), "freeing the simulation graph");
+        if (stream_) checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream_), "freeing the simulation stream");
+        if (storage_) checkCuda(cudaFree(storage_), "freeing body state");
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    void run(int steps) {
+        if (n_ == 0 || steps <= 0) return;
+        while (graph_ && positions_ == storage_ && steps >= graphSteps_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(graph_, stream_), "launching the simulation graph");
+            steps -= graphSteps_;
+        }
+        for (int step = 0; step < steps; ++step) advance();
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the simulation kernels");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_), "running the simulation");
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data(), positions_, 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data() + 3 * stride_, velocities_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "downloading velocities");
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(state_[i], state_[stride_ + i], state_[2 * stride_ + i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(state_[3 * stride_ + i], state_[4 * stride_ + i],
+                                 state_[5 * stride_ + i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    void advance() {
+        // Smaller blocks expose more independent bodies for small problems.
+        if (n_ < 4096) {
+            launch<32>();
+        } else if (n_ < 16384) {
+            launch<64>();
+        } else {
+            launch<128>();
+        }
+        std::swap(positions_, nextPositions_);
+    }
+
+    template <int BlockSize>
+    void launch() {
+        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>((n_ + BlockSize - 1) / BlockSize);
+        advanceBodies<BlockSize><<<blocks, BlockSize, 0, stream_>>>(positions_, nextPositions_, velocities_, n_, stride_);
+    }
+
+    const size_t n_, stride_;
+    std::vector<double> state_;
+    double* storage_ = nullptr;
+    double* positions_ = nullptr;
+    double* nextPositions_ = nullptr;
+    double* velocities_ = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t graph_ = nullptr;
+    int graphSteps_ = 0;
+};
+

@@ -158,17 +304,16 @@

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }

+// Each thread owns one target body. Source positions are loaded cooperatively
+// into shared memory and reused by all targets in the block.
+template <int BlockSize>
+__global__ void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positions,
+                              double* __restrict__ nextPositions,
+                              double* __restrict__ velocities,
+                              size_t n, size_t stride) {
+    __shared__ double tile[3][BlockSize];
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * BlockSize + lane;
+    const bool active = i < n;
+    const double px = active ? positions[i] : 0.0;
+    const double py = active ? positions[stride + i] : 0.0;
+    const double pz = active ? positions[2 * stride + i] : 0.0;
+    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < n; base += BlockSize) {
+        const size_t j = base + lane;
+        tile[0][lane] = j < n ? positions[j] : 0.0;
+        tile[1][lane] = j < n ? positions[stride + j] : 0.0;
+        tile[2][lane] = j < n ? positions[2 * stride + j] : 0.0;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(n - base < BlockSize ? n - base : BlockSize);
+        if (active) {
+            // Preserve increasing source-index order; no parallel force reduction
+            // or reduced-precision/approximate reciprocal square root is used.
+            #pragma unroll 4
+            for (int k = 0; k < count; ++k) {
+                const double dx = tile[0][k] - px;
+                const double dy = tile[1][k] - py;
+                const double dz = tile[2][k] - pz;
+                const double distSqr = __fma_rn(dz, dz, __fma_rn(dx, dx, dy * dy)) + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+                fx = __fma_rn(dx, invDist3, fx);
+                fy = __fma_rn(dy, invDist3, fy);
+                fz = __fma_rn(dz, invDist3, fz);
+            }
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        // Inactive threads in a partial block must also reach both barriers.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-}
 
-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+    if (active) {
+        const double vx = __dadd_rn(velocities[i], __dmul_rn(DT, fx));
+        const double vy = __dadd_rn(velocities[stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fy));
+        const double vz = __dadd_rn(velocities[2 * stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fz));
+        velocities[i] = vx;
+        velocities[stride + i] = vy;
+        velocities[2 * stride + i] = vz;
+        // A separate output buffer keeps every force calculation on the same
+        // position snapshot, even though integration is fused into this kernel.
+        nextPositions[i] = __fma_rn(vx, DT, px);
+        nextPositions[stride + i] = __fma_rn(vy, DT, py);
+        nextPositions[2 * stride + i] = __fma_rn(vz, DT, pz);
     }
 }
