+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, int steps)
+        : n_(bodies.size()), stride_((n_ + 31) / 32 * 32), state_(6 * stride_) {
+        // CUDA is required even for an empty simulation; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "initializing the CUDA runtime");
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            state_[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            state_[stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            state_[2 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            state_[3 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            state_[4 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            state_[5 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&storage_), 9 * stride_ * sizeof(double)),
+                  "allocating body state");
+        positions_ = storage_;
+        nextPositions_ = storage_ + 3 * stride_;
+        velocities_ = storage_ + 6 * stride_;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_, state_.data(), 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "uploading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, state_.data() + 3 * stride_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "uploading velocities");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "creating the simulation stream");
+
+        if (steps >= 4) {
+            // An even batch returns to the same position buffer, so this graph
+            // can be replayed without updating any device pointers or nodes.
+            graphSteps_ = steps < 32 ? steps / 2 * 2 : 32;
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream_, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                      "capturing the simulation graph");
+            for (int step = 0; step < graphSteps_; ++step) advance();
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "capturing simulation kernels");
+            cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream_, &graph), "finishing the simulation graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                      "instantiating the simulation graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "freeing the captured graph");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (graph_) checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(graph_), "freeing the simulation graph");
+        if (stream_) checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream_), "freeing the simulation stream");
+        if (storage_) checkCuda(cudaFree(storage_), "freeing body state");
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    void run(int steps) {
+        if (n_ == 0 || steps <= 0) return;
+        while (graph_ && positions_ == storage_ && steps >= graphSteps_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(graph_, stream_), "launching the simulation graph");
+            steps -= graphSteps_;
+        }
+        for (int step = 0; step < steps; ++step) advance();
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the simulation kernels");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_), "running the simulation");
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data(), positions_, 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data() + 3 * stride_, velocities_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "downloading velocities");
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(state_[i], state_[stride_ + i], state_[2 * stride_ + i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(state_[3 * stride_ + i], state_[4 * stride_ + i],
+                                 state_[5 * stride_ + i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    void advance() {
+        // Smaller blocks expose more independent bodies for small problems.
+        if (n_ < 4096) {
+            launch<32>();
+        } else if (n_ < 16384) {
+            launch<64>();
+        } else {
+            launch<128>();
+        }
+        std::swap(positions_, nextPositions_);
+    }
+
+    template <int BlockSize>
+    void launch() {
+        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>((n_ + BlockSize - 1) / BlockSize);
+        advanceBodies<BlockSize><<<blocks, BlockSize, 0, stream_>>>(positions_, nextPositions_, velocities_, n_, stride_);
+    }
+
+    const size_t n_, stride_;
+    std::vector<double> state_;
+    double* storage_ = nullptr;
+    double* positions_ = nullptr;
+    double* nextPositions_ = nullptr;
+    double* velocities_ = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t graph_ = nullptr;
+    int graphSteps_ = 0;
+};
+

@@ -158,17 +304,16 @@

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }

+// Each thread owns one target body. Source positions are loaded cooperatively
+// into shared memory and reused by all targets in the block.
+template <int BlockSize>
+__global__ void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positions,
+                              double* __restrict__ nextPositions,
+                              double* __restrict__ velocities,
+                              size_t n, size_t stride) {
+    __shared__ double tile[3][BlockSize];
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * BlockSize + lane;
+    const bool active = i < n;
+    const double px = active ? positions[i] : 0.0;
+    const double py = active ? positions[stride + i] : 0.0;
+    const double pz = active ? positions[2 * stride + i] : 0.0;
+    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < n; base += BlockSize) {
+        const size_t j = base + lane;
+        tile[0][lane] = j < n ? positions[j] : 0.0;
+        tile[1][lane] = j < n ? positions[stride + j] : 0.0;
+        tile[2][lane] = j < n ? positions[2 * stride + j] : 0.0;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(n - base < BlockSize ? n - base : BlockSize);
+        if (active) {
+            // Preserve increasing source-index order; no parallel force reduction
+            // or reduced-precision/approximate reciprocal square root is used.
+            #pragma unroll 4
+            for (int k = 0; k < count; ++k) {
+                const double dx = tile[0][k] - px;
+                const double dy = tile[1][k] - py;
+                const double dz = tile[2][k] - pz;
+                const double distSqr = __fma_rn(dz, dz, __fma_rn(dx, dx, dy * dy)) + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+                fx = __fma_rn(dx, invDist3, fx);
+                fy = __fma_rn(dy, invDist3, fy);
+                fz = __fma_rn(dz, invDist3, fz);
+            }
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        // Inactive threads in a partial block must also reach both barriers.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-}

+        velocities[2 * stride + i] = vz;

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));

+// Each thread owns one target body. Source positions are loaded cooperatively
+// into shared memory and reused by all targets in the block.
+template <int BlockSize>
+__global__ void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positions,
+                              double* __restrict__ nextPositions,
+                              double* __restrict__ velocities,
+                              size_t n, size_t stride) {
+    __shared__ double tile[3][BlockSize];
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * BlockSize + lane;
+    const bool active = i < n;
+    const double px = active ? positions[i] : 0.0;
+    const double py = active ? positions[stride + i] : 0.0;
+    const double pz = active ? positions[2 * stride + i] : 0.0;
+    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < n; base += BlockSize) {
+        const size_t j = base + lane;
+        tile[0][lane] = j < n ? positions[j] : 0.0;
+        tile[1][lane] = j < n ? positions[stride + j] : 0.0;
+        tile[2][lane] = j < n ? positions[2 * stride + j] : 0.0;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(n - base < BlockSize ? n - base : BlockSize);
+        if (active) {
+            // Preserve increasing source-index order; no parallel force reduction
+            // or reduced-precision/approximate reciprocal square root is used.
+            #pragma unroll 4
+            for (int k = 0; k < count; ++k) {
+                const double dx = tile[0][k] - px;
+                const double dy = tile[1][k] - py;
+                const double dz = tile[2][k] - pz;
+                const double distSqr = __fma_rn(dz, dz, __fma_rn(dx, dx, dy * dy)) + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+                fx = __fma_rn(dx, invDist3, fx);
+                fy = __fma_rn(dy, invDist3, fy);
+                fz = __fma_rn(dz, invDist3, fz);
+            }
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        // Inactive threads in a partial block must also reach both barriers.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-}
 
-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {

-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+    if (active) {
+        const double vx = __dadd_rn(velocities[i], __dmul_rn(DT, fx));
+        const double vy = __dadd_rn(velocities[stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fy));
+        const double vz = __dadd_rn(velocities[2 * stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fz));
+        velocities[i] = vx;
+        velocities[stride + i] = vy;
+        velocities[2 * stride + i] = vz;
+        // A separate output buffer keeps every force calculation on the same
+        // position snapshot, even though integration is fused into this kernel.
+        nextPositions[i] = __fma_rn(vx, DT, px);
+        nextPositions[stride + i] = __fma_rn(vy, DT, py);
+        nextPositions[2 * stride + i] = __fma_rn(vz, DT, pz);
     }
 }
 
+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, int steps)
+        : n_(bodies.size()), stride_((n_ + 31) / 32 * 32), state_(6 * stride_) {
+        // CUDA is required even for an empty simulation; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "initializing the CUDA runtime");
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            state_[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            state_[stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            state_[2 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            state_[3 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            state_[4 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            state_[5 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&storage_), 9 * stride_ * sizeof(double)),
+                  "allocating body state");
+        positions_ = storage_;
+        nextPositions_ = storage_ + 3 * stride_;
+        velocities_ = storage_ + 6 * stride_;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_, state_.data(), 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "uploading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, state_.data() + 3 * stride_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "uploading velocities");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "creating the simulation stream");
+
+        if (steps >= 4) {
+            // An even batch returns to the same position buffer, so this graph
+            // can be replayed without updating any device pointers or nodes.
+            graphSteps_ = steps < 32 ? steps / 2 * 2 : 32;
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream_, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                      "capturing the simulation graph");
+            for (int step = 0; step < graphSteps_; ++step) advance();
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "capturing simulation kernels");
+            cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream_, &graph), "finishing the simulation graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                      "instantiating the simulation graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "freeing the captured graph");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (graph_) checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(graph_), "freeing the simulation graph");
+        if (stream_) checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream_), "freeing the simulation stream");
+        if (storage_) checkCuda(cudaFree(storage_), "freeing body state");
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    void run(int steps) {
+        if (n_ == 0 || steps <= 0) return;
+        while (graph_ && positions_ == storage_ && steps >= graphSteps_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(graph_, stream_), "launching the simulation graph");
+            steps -= graphSteps_;
+        }
+        for (int step = 0; step < steps; ++step) advance();
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the simulation kernels");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_), "running the simulation");
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data(), positions_, 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data() + 3 * stride_, velocities_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "downloading velocities");
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(state_[i], state_[stride_ + i], state_[2 * stride_ + i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(state_[3 * stride_ + i], state_[4 * stride_ + i],
+                                 state_[5 * stride_ + i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    void advance() {
+        // Smaller blocks expose more independent bodies for small problems.
+        if (n_ < 4096) {
+            launch<32>();
+        } else if (n_ < 16384) {
+            launch<64>();
+        } else {
+            launch<128>();
+        }
+        std::swap(positions_, nextPositions_);
+    }
+
+    template <int BlockSize>
+    void launch() {
+        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>((n_ + BlockSize - 1) / BlockSize);
+        advanceBodies<BlockSize><<<blocks, BlockSize, 0, stream_>>>(positions_, nextPositions_, velocities_, n_, stride_);
+    }
+
+    const size_t n_, stride_;
+    std::vector<double> state_;
+    double* storage_ = nullptr;
+    double* positions_ = nullptr;
+    double* nextPositions_ = nullptr;
+    double* velocities_ = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t graph_ = nullptr;
+    int graphSteps_ = 0;
+};
+

     const size_t n = bodies.size();

+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, int steps)
+        : n_(bodies.size()), stride_((n_ + 31) / 32 * 32), state_(6 * stride_) {
+        // CUDA is required even for an empty simulation; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "initializing the CUDA runtime");
+        if (n_ == 0) return;

+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            state_[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            state_[stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            state_[2 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            state_[3 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            state_[4 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            state_[5 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&storage_), 9 * stride_ * sizeof(double)),
+                  "allocating body state");
+        positions_ = storage_;
+        nextPositions_ = storage_ + 3 * stride_;
+        velocities_ = storage_ + 6 * stride_;

+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "uploading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, state_.data() + 3 * stride_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "uploading velocities");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "creating the simulation stream");

+        if (storage_) checkCuda(cudaFree(storage_), "freeing body state");

+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;

+    void run(int steps) {

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+// Each thread owns one target body. Source positions are loaded cooperatively
+// into shared memory and reused by all targets in the block.
+template <int BlockSize>
+__global__ void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positions,
+                              double* __restrict__ nextPositions,
+                              double* __restrict__ velocities,
+                              size_t n, size_t stride) {
+    __shared__ double tile[3][BlockSize];
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * BlockSize + lane;
+    const bool active = i < n;
+    const double px = active ? positions[i] : 0.0;
+    const double py = active ? positions[stride + i] : 0.0;
+    const double pz = active ? positions[2 * stride + i] : 0.0;
+    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < n; base += BlockSize) {
+        const size_t j = base + lane;
+        tile[0][lane] = j < n ? positions[j] : 0.0;
+        tile[1][lane] = j < n ? positions[stride + j] : 0.0;
+        tile[2][lane] = j < n ? positions[2 * stride + j] : 0.0;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(n - base < BlockSize ? n - base : BlockSize);
+        if (active) {
+            // Preserve increasing source-index order; no parallel force reduction
+            // or reduced-precision/approximate reciprocal square root is used.
+            #pragma unroll 4
+            for (int k = 0; k < count; ++k) {
+                const double dx = tile[0][k] - px;
+                const double dy = tile[1][k] - py;
+                const double dz = tile[2][k] - pz;
+                const double distSqr = __fma_rn(dz, dz, __fma_rn(dx, dx, dy * dy)) + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+                fx = __fma_rn(dx, invDist3, fx);
+                fy = __fma_rn(dy, invDist3, fy);
+                fz = __fma_rn(dz, invDist3, fz);
+            }
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        // Inactive threads in a partial block must also reach both barriers.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-}
 
-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {

-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+    if (active) {
+        const double vx = __dadd_rn(velocities[i], __dmul_rn(DT, fx));
+        const double vy = __dadd_rn(velocities[stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fy));
+        const double vz = __dadd_rn(velocities[2 * stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fz));
+        velocities[i] = vx;
+        velocities[stride + i] = vy;
+        velocities[2 * stride + i] = vz;
+        // A separate output buffer keeps every force calculation on the same
+        // position snapshot, even though integration is fused into this kernel.
+        nextPositions[i] = __fma_rn(vx, DT, px);
+        nextPositions[stride + i] = __fma_rn(vy, DT, py);
+        nextPositions[2 * stride + i] = __fma_rn(vz, DT, pz);
     }
 }
 
+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, int steps)
+        : n_(bodies.size()), stride_((n_ + 31) / 32 * 32), state_(6 * stride_) {
+        // CUDA is required even for an empty simulation; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "initializing the CUDA runtime");
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            state_[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            state_[stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            state_[2 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            state_[3 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            state_[4 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            state_[5 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&storage_), 9 * stride_ * sizeof(double)),
+                  "allocating body state");
+        positions_ = storage_;
+        nextPositions_ = storage_ + 3 * stride_;
+        velocities_ = storage_ + 6 * stride_;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_, state_.data(), 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "uploading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, state_.data() + 3 * stride_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "uploading velocities");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "creating the simulation stream");
+
+        if (steps >= 4) {
+            // An even batch returns to the same position buffer, so this graph
+            // can be replayed without updating any device pointers or nodes.
+            graphSteps_ = steps < 32 ? steps / 2 * 2 : 32;
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream_, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                      "capturing the simulation graph");
+            for (int step = 0; step < graphSteps_; ++step) advance();
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "capturing simulation kernels");
+            cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream_, &graph), "finishing the simulation graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                      "instantiating the simulation graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "freeing the captured graph");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (graph_) checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(graph_), "freeing the simulation graph");
+        if (stream_) checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream_), "freeing the simulation stream");
+        if (storage_) checkCuda(cudaFree(storage_), "freeing body state");
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    void run(int steps) {
+        if (n_ == 0 || steps <= 0) return;
+        while (graph_ && positions_ == storage_ && steps >= graphSteps_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(graph_, stream_), "launching the simulation graph");
+            steps -= graphSteps_;
+        }
+        for (int step = 0; step < steps; ++step) advance();
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the simulation kernels");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_), "running the simulation");
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data(), positions_, 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data() + 3 * stride_, velocities_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "downloading velocities");
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(state_[i], state_[stride_ + i], state_[2 * stride_ + i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(state_[3 * stride_ + i], state_[4 * stride_ + i],
+                                 state_[5 * stride_ + i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    void advance() {
+        // Smaller blocks expose more independent bodies for small problems.
+        if (n_ < 4096) {
+            launch<32>();
+        } else if (n_ < 16384) {
+            launch<64>();
+        } else {
+            launch<128>();
+        }
+        std::swap(positions_, nextPositions_);
+    }
+
+    template <int BlockSize>
+    void launch() {
+        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>((n_ + BlockSize - 1) / BlockSize);
+        advanceBodies<BlockSize><<<blocks, BlockSize, 0, stream_>>>(positions_, nextPositions_, velocities_, n_, stride_);
+    }
+
+    const size_t n_, stride_;
+    std::vector<double> state_;
+    double* storage_ = nullptr;
+    double* positions_ = nullptr;
+    double* nextPositions_ = nullptr;
+    double* velocities_ = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t graph_ = nullptr;
+    int graphSteps_ = 0;
+};
+

     const size_t n = bodies.size();

+            state_[2 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            state_[3 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            state_[4 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            state_[5 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&storage_), 9 * stride_ * sizeof(double)),
+                  "allocating body state");
+        positions_ = storage_;
+        nextPositions_ = storage_ + 3 * stride_;
+        velocities_ = storage_ + 6 * stride_;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_, state_.data(), 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "uploading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, state_.data() + 3 * stride_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "uploading velocities");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "creating the simulation stream");
+
+        if (steps >= 4) {
+            // An even batch returns to the same position buffer, so this graph
+            // can be replayed without updating any device pointers or nodes.
+            graphSteps_ = steps < 32 ? steps / 2 * 2 : 32;
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream_, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                      "capturing the simulation graph");
+            for (int step = 0; step < graphSteps_; ++step) advance();
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "capturing simulation kernels");
+            cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream_, &graph), "finishing the simulation graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                      "instantiating the simulation graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "freeing the captured graph");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (graph_) checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(graph_), "freeing the simulation graph");
+        if (stream_) checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream_), "freeing the simulation stream");
+        if (storage_) checkCuda(cudaFree(storage_), "freeing body state");
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    void run(int steps) {
+        if (n_ == 0 || steps <= 0) return;
+        while (graph_ && positions_ == storage_ && steps >= graphSteps_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(graph_, stream_), "launching the simulation graph");
+            steps -= graphSteps_;
+        }
+        for (int step = 0; step < steps; ++step) advance();
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the simulation kernels");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_), "running the simulation");
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data(), positions_, 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data() + 3 * stride_, velocities_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "downloading velocities");
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(state_[i], state_[stride_ + i], state_[2 * stride_ + i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(state_[3 * stride_ + i], state_[4 * stride_ + i],
+                                 state_[5 * stride_ + i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    void advance() {
+        // Smaller blocks expose more independent bodies for small problems.
+        if (n_ < 4096) {
+            launch<32>();
+        } else if (n_ < 16384) {
+            launch<64>();
+        } else {
+            launch<128>();
+        }
+        std::swap(positions_, nextPositions_);
+    }
+
+    template <int BlockSize>
+    void launch() {
+        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>((n_ + BlockSize - 1) / BlockSize);
+        advanceBodies<BlockSize><<<blocks, BlockSize, 0, stream_>>>(positions_, nextPositions_, velocities_, n_, stride_);
+    }
+
+    const size_t n_, stride_;
+    std::vector<double> state_;
+    double* storage_ = nullptr;
+    double* positions_ = nullptr;
+    double* nextPositions_ = nullptr;
+    double* velocities_ = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t graph_ = nullptr;
+    int graphSteps_ = 0;
+};

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+// Each thread owns one target body. Source positions are loaded cooperatively
+// into shared memory and reused by all targets in the block.
+template <int BlockSize>
+__global__ void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positions,
+                              double* __restrict__ nextPositions,
+                              double* __restrict__ velocities,
+                              size_t n, size_t stride) {
+    __shared__ double tile[3][BlockSize];
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * BlockSize + lane;
+    const bool active = i < n;
+    const double px = active ? positions[i] : 0.0;
+    const double py = active ? positions[stride + i] : 0.0;
+    const double pz = active ? positions[2 * stride + i] : 0.0;
+    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < n; base += BlockSize) {
+        const size_t j = base + lane;
+        tile[0][lane] = j < n ? positions[j] : 0.0;
+        tile[1][lane] = j < n ? positions[stride + j] : 0.0;
+        tile[2][lane] = j < n ? positions[2 * stride + j] : 0.0;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(n - base < BlockSize ? n - base : BlockSize);
+        if (active) {
+            // Preserve increasing source-index order; no parallel force reduction
+            // or reduced-precision/approximate reciprocal square root is used.
+            #pragma unroll 4
+            for (int k = 0; k < count; ++k) {
+                const double dx = tile[0][k] - px;
+                const double dy = tile[1][k] - py;
+                const double dz = tile[2][k] - pz;
+                const double distSqr = __fma_rn(dz, dz, __fma_rn(dx, dx, dy * dy)) + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+                fx = __fma_rn(dx, invDist3, fx);
+                fy = __fma_rn(dy, invDist3, fy);
+                fz = __fma_rn(dz, invDist3, fz);
+            }
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        // Inactive threads in a partial block must also reach both barriers.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (active) {
+        const double vx = __dadd_rn(velocities[i], __dmul_rn(DT, fx));
+        const double vy = __dadd_rn(velocities[stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fy));
+        const double vz = __dadd_rn(velocities[2 * stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fz));
+        velocities[i] = vx;
+        velocities[stride + i] = vy;
+        velocities[2 * stride + i] = vz;
+        // A separate output buffer keeps every force calculation on the same
+        // position snapshot, even though integration is fused into this kernel.
+        nextPositions[i] = __fma_rn(vx, DT, px);
+        nextPositions[stride + i] = __fma_rn(vy, DT, py);
+        nextPositions[2 * stride + i] = __fma_rn(vz, DT, pz);
+    }
 }

+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, int steps)
+        : n_(bodies.size()), stride_((n_ + 31) / 32 * 32), state_(6 * stride_) {
+        // CUDA is required even for an empty simulation; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "initializing the CUDA runtime");
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            state_[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            state_[stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            state_[2 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            state_[3 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;

+            state_[5 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }

+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&storage_), 9 * stride_ * sizeof(double)),
+                  "allocating body state");
+        positions_ = storage_;
+        nextPositions_ = storage_ + 3 * stride_;
+        velocities_ = storage_ + 6 * stride_;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_, state_.data(), 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "uploading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, state_.data() + 3 * stride_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "uploading velocities");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "creating the simulation stream");
+
+        if (steps >= 4) {
+            // An even batch returns to the same position buffer, so this graph
+            // can be replayed without updating any device pointers or nodes.
+            graphSteps_ = steps < 32 ? steps / 2 * 2 : 32;
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream_, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                      "capturing the simulation graph");
+            for (int step = 0; step < graphSteps_; ++step) advance();
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "capturing simulation kernels");
+            cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream_, &graph), "finishing the simulation graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                      "instantiating the simulation graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "freeing the captured graph");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (graph_) checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(graph_), "freeing the simulation graph");
+        if (stream_) checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream_), "freeing the simulation stream");
+        if (storage_) checkCuda(cudaFree(storage_), "freeing body state");
     }

+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;

+        if (n_ == 0 || steps <= 0) return;
+        while (graph_ && positions_ == storage_ && steps >= graphSteps_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(graph_, stream_), "launching the simulation graph");
+            steps -= graphSteps_;
+        }
+        for (int step = 0; step < steps; ++step) advance();
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the simulation kernels");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_), "running the simulation");
+    }

+        // One warp per block exposes many independent targets and avoids
+        // waiting for other warps at each shared-memory tile barrier.
+        constexpr int BlockSize = 32;

+        advanceBodies<BlockSize><<<blocks, BlockSize, 0, stream_>>>(positions_, nextPositions_, velocities_, n_, stride_);

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+// Each thread owns one target body. Source positions are loaded cooperatively
+// into shared memory and reused by all targets in the block.
+template <int BlockSize>
+__global__ void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positions,
+                              double* __restrict__ nextPositions,
+                              double* __restrict__ velocities,
+                              size_t n, size_t stride) {
+    __shared__ double tile[3][BlockSize];
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * BlockSize + lane;
+    const bool active = i < n;
+    const double px = active ? positions[i] : 0.0;
+    const double py = active ? positions[stride + i] : 0.0;
+    const double pz = active ? positions[2 * stride + i] : 0.0;
+    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < n; base += BlockSize) {
+        const size_t j = base + lane;
+        tile[0][lane] = j < n ? positions[j] : 0.0;
+        tile[1][lane] = j < n ? positions[stride + j] : 0.0;
+        tile[2][lane] = j < n ? positions[2 * stride + j] : 0.0;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(n - base < BlockSize ? n - base : BlockSize);
+        if (active) {
+            // Preserve increasing source-index order; no parallel force reduction
+            // or reduced-precision/approximate reciprocal square root is used.
+            #pragma unroll 4
+            for (int k = 0; k < count; ++k) {
+                const double dx = tile[0][k] - px;
+                const double dy = tile[1][k] - py;
+                const double dz = tile[2][k] - pz;
+                const double distSqr = __fma_rn(dz, dz, __fma_rn(dx, dx, dy * dy)) + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+                fx = __fma_rn(dx, invDist3, fx);
+                fy = __fma_rn(dy, invDist3, fy);
+                fz = __fma_rn(dz, invDist3, fz);
+            }
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        // Inactive threads in a partial block must also reach both barriers.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (active) {
+        const double vx = __dadd_rn(velocities[i], __dmul_rn(DT, fx));
+        const double vy = __dadd_rn(velocities[stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fy));
+        const double vz = __dadd_rn(velocities[2 * stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fz));
+        velocities[i] = vx;
+        velocities[stride + i] = vy;
+        velocities[2 * stride + i] = vz;
+        // A separate output buffer keeps every force calculation on the same
+        // position snapshot, even though integration is fused into this kernel.
+        nextPositions[i] = __fma_rn(vx, DT, px);
+        nextPositions[stride + i] = __fma_rn(vy, DT, py);
+        nextPositions[2 * stride + i] = __fma_rn(vz, DT, pz);
+    }
 }

+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+// Each thread owns one target body. Source positions are loaded cooperatively
+// into shared memory and reused by all targets in the block.
+template <int BlockSize>
+__global__ void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positions,
+                              double* __restrict__ nextPositions,
+                              double* __restrict__ velocities,
+                              size_t n, size_t stride) {
+    __shared__ double tile[3][BlockSize];
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * BlockSize + lane;
+    const bool active = i < n;
+    const double px = active ? positions[i] : 0.0;
+    const double py = active ? positions[stride + i] : 0.0;
+    const double pz = active ? positions[2 * stride + i] : 0.0;
+    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < n; base += BlockSize) {
+        const size_t j = base + lane;
+        tile[0][lane] = j < n ? positions[j] : 0.0;
+        tile[1][lane] = j < n ? positions[stride + j] : 0.0;
+        tile[2][lane] = j < n ? positions[2 * stride + j] : 0.0;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(n - base < BlockSize ? n - base : BlockSize);
+        if (active) {
+            // Preserve increasing source-index order; no parallel force reduction
+            // or reduced-precision/approximate reciprocal square root is used.
+            #pragma unroll 4
+            for (int k = 0; k < count; ++k) {
+                const double dx = tile[0][k] - px;
+                const double dy = tile[1][k] - py;
+                const double dz = tile[2][k] - pz;
+                const double distSqr = __fma_rn(dz, dz, __fma_rn(dx, dx, dy * dy)) + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+                fx = __fma_rn(dx, invDist3, fx);
+                fy = __fma_rn(dy, invDist3, fy);
+                fz = __fma_rn(dz, invDist3, fz);
+            }
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        // Inactive threads in a partial block must also reach both barriers.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (active) {
+        const double vx = __dadd_rn(velocities[i], __dmul_rn(DT, fx));
+        const double vy = __dadd_rn(velocities[stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fy));
+        const double vz = __dadd_rn(velocities[2 * stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fz));
+        velocities[i] = vx;
+        velocities[stride + i] = vy;
+        velocities[2 * stride + i] = vz;
+        // A separate output buffer keeps every force calculation on the same
+        // position snapshot, even though integration is fused into this kernel.
+        nextPositions[i] = __fma_rn(vx, DT, px);
+        nextPositions[stride + i] = __fma_rn(vy, DT, py);
+        nextPositions[2 * stride + i] = __fma_rn(vz, DT, pz);
+    }
 }

+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, int steps)
+        : n_(bodies.size()), stride_((n_ + 31) / 32 * 32), state_(6 * stride_) {
+        // CUDA is required even for an empty simulation; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "initializing the CUDA runtime");
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            state_[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            state_[stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            state_[2 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            state_[3 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            state_[4 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            state_[5 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&storage_), 9 * stride_ * sizeof(double)),
+                  "allocating body state");
+        positions_ = storage_;
+        nextPositions_ = storage_ + 3 * stride_;
+        velocities_ = storage_ + 6 * stride_;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_, state_.data(), 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "uploading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, state_.data() + 3 * stride_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "uploading velocities");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "creating the simulation stream");
+
+        if (steps >= 4) {
+            // An even batch returns to the same position buffer, so this graph
+            // can be replayed without updating any device pointers or nodes.
+            graphSteps_ = steps < 32 ? steps / 2 * 2 : 32;
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream_, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                      "capturing the simulation graph");
+            for (int step = 0; step < graphSteps_; ++step) advance();
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "capturing simulation kernels");
+            cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream_, &graph), "finishing the simulation graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                      "instantiating the simulation graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "freeing the captured graph");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (graph_) checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(graph_), "freeing the simulation graph");
+        if (stream_) checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream_), "freeing the simulation stream");
+        if (storage_) checkCuda(cudaFree(storage_), "freeing body state");
     }
-}
 
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    void run(int steps) {
+        if (n_ == 0 || steps <= 0) return;
+        while (graph_ && positions_ == storage_ && steps >= graphSteps_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(graph_, stream_), "launching the simulation graph");
+            steps -= graphSteps_;
+        }
+        for (int step = 0; step < steps; ++step) advance();
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the simulation kernels");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_), "running the simulation");
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data(), positions_, 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data() + 3 * stride_, velocities_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "downloading velocities");
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(state_[i], state_[stride_ + i], state_[2 * stride_ + i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(state_[3 * stride_ + i], state_[4 * stride_ + i],
+                                 state_[5 * stride_ + i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    void advance() {
+        // One warp per block exposes many independent targets and avoids
+        // waiting for other warps at each shared-memory tile barrier.
+        constexpr int BlockSize = 32;
+        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>((n_ + BlockSize - 1) / BlockSize);
+        advanceBodies<BlockSize><<<blocks, BlockSize, 0, stream_>>>(positions_, nextPositions_, velocities_, n_, stride_);
+        std::swap(positions_, nextPositions_);
+    }
+
+    const size_t n_, stride_;
+    std::vector<double> state_;
+    double* storage_ = nullptr;
+    double* positions_ = nullptr;
+    double* nextPositions_ = nullptr;
+    double* velocities_ = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t graph_ = nullptr;
+    int graphSteps_ = 0;
+};

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+// Each thread owns one target body. Source positions are loaded cooperatively
+// into shared memory and reused by all targets in the block.
+template <int BlockSize, int Unroll>
+__global__ void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positions,
+                              double* __restrict__ nextPositions,
+                              double* __restrict__ velocities,
+                              size_t n, size_t stride) {
+    __shared__ double tile[3][BlockSize];
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * BlockSize + lane;
+    const bool active = i < n;
+    const double px = active ? positions[i] : 0.0;
+    const double py = active ? positions[stride + i] : 0.0;
+    const double pz = active ? positions[2 * stride + i] : 0.0;
+    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < n; base += BlockSize) {
+        const size_t j = base + lane;
+        tile[0][lane] = j < n ? positions[j] : 0.0;
+        tile[1][lane] = j < n ? positions[stride + j] : 0.0;
+        tile[2][lane] = j < n ? positions[2 * stride + j] : 0.0;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(n - base < BlockSize ? n - base : BlockSize);
+        if (active) {
+            // Preserve increasing source-index order; no parallel force reduction
+            // or reduced-precision/approximate reciprocal square root is used.
+            #pragma unroll Unroll
+            for (int k = 0; k < count; ++k) {
+                const double dx = tile[0][k] - px;
+                const double dy = tile[1][k] - py;
+                const double dz = tile[2][k] - pz;
+                const double distSqr = __fma_rn(dz, dz, __fma_rn(dx, dx, dy * dy)) + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+                fx = __fma_rn(dx, invDist3, fx);
+                fy = __fma_rn(dy, invDist3, fy);
+                fz = __fma_rn(dz, invDist3, fz);
+            }
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        // Inactive threads in a partial block must also reach both barriers.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (active) {
+        const double vx = __dadd_rn(velocities[i], __dmul_rn(DT, fx));
+        const double vy = __dadd_rn(velocities[stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fy));
+        const double vz = __dadd_rn(velocities[2 * stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fz));
+        velocities[i] = vx;
+        velocities[stride + i] = vy;
+        velocities[2 * stride + i] = vz;
+        // A separate output buffer keeps every force calculation on the same
+        // position snapshot, even though integration is fused into this kernel.
+        nextPositions[i] = __fma_rn(vx, DT, px);
+        nextPositions[stride + i] = __fma_rn(vy, DT, py);
+        nextPositions[2 * stride + i] = __fma_rn(vz, DT, pz);
+    }
 }

+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, int steps)
+        : n_(bodies.size()), stride_((n_ + 31) / 32 * 32), state_(6 * stride_) {
+        // CUDA is required even for an empty simulation; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "initializing the CUDA runtime");
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            state_[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            state_[stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            state_[2 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            state_[3 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            state_[4 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            state_[5 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&storage_), 9 * stride_ * sizeof(double)),
+                  "allocating body state");
+        positions_ = storage_;
+        nextPositions_ = storage_ + 3 * stride_;
+        velocities_ = storage_ + 6 * stride_;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_, state_.data(), 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "uploading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, state_.data() + 3 * stride_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "uploading velocities");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "creating the simulation stream");
+
+        if (steps >= 4) {
+            // An even batch returns to the same position buffer, so this graph
+            // can be replayed without updating any device pointers or nodes.
+            graphSteps_ = steps < 32 ? steps / 2 * 2 : 32;
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream_, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                      "capturing the simulation graph");
+            for (int step = 0; step < graphSteps_; ++step) advance();
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "capturing simulation kernels");
+            cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream_, &graph), "finishing the simulation graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                      "instantiating the simulation graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "freeing the captured graph");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (graph_) checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(graph_), "freeing the simulation graph");
+        if (stream_) checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream_), "freeing the simulation stream");
+        if (storage_) checkCuda(cudaFree(storage_), "freeing body state");
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    void run(int steps) {
+        if (n_ == 0 || steps <= 0) return;
+        while (graph_ && positions_ == storage_ && steps >= graphSteps_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(graph_, stream_), "launching the simulation graph");
+            steps -= graphSteps_;
+        }
+        for (int step = 0; step < steps; ++step) advance();
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the simulation kernels");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_), "running the simulation");
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data(), positions_, 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data() + 3 * stride_, velocities_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "downloading velocities");
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(state_[i], state_[stride_ + i], state_[2 * stride_ + i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(state_[3 * stride_ + i], state_[4 * stride_ + i],
+                                 state_[5 * stride_ + i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    void advance() {
+        // One warp per block exposes many independent targets and avoids
+        // waiting for other warps at each shared-memory tile barrier.
+        constexpr int BlockSize = 32;
+        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>((n_ + BlockSize - 1) / BlockSize);
+        if (n_ < 4096) {
+            advanceBodies<BlockSize, 16><<<blocks, BlockSize, 0, stream_>>>(positions_, nextPositions_, velocities_, n_, stride_);
+        } else {
+            advanceBodies<BlockSize, 4><<<blocks, BlockSize, 0, stream_>>>(positions_, nextPositions_, velocities_, n_, stride_);
+        }
+        std::swap(positions_, nextPositions_);
     }
-}
 
+    const size_t n_, stride_;
+    std::vector<double> state_;
+    double* storage_ = nullptr;
+    double* positions_ = nullptr;
+    double* nextPositions_ = nullptr;
+    double* velocities_ = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t graph_ = nullptr;
+    int graphSteps_ = 0;
+};

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));

+// Each thread owns one target body. Source positions are loaded cooperatively
+// into shared memory and reused by all targets in the block.
+template <int BlockSize, int Unroll>
+__global__ void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positions,
+                              double* __restrict__ nextPositions,
+                              double* __restrict__ velocities,
+                              size_t n, size_t stride) {
+    __shared__ double tile[3][BlockSize];
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * BlockSize + lane;
+    const bool active = i < n;
+    const double px = active ? positions[i] : 0.0;
+    const double py = active ? positions[stride + i] : 0.0;
+    const double pz = active ? positions[2 * stride + i] : 0.0;
+    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < n; base += BlockSize) {
+        const size_t j = base + lane;
+        tile[0][lane] = j < n ? positions[j] : 0.0;
+        tile[1][lane] = j < n ? positions[stride + j] : 0.0;
+        tile[2][lane] = j < n ? positions[2 * stride + j] : 0.0;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(n - base < BlockSize ? n - base : BlockSize);
+        if (active) {
+            // Preserve increasing source-index order; no parallel force reduction
+            // or reduced-precision/approximate reciprocal square root is used.
+            #pragma unroll Unroll
+            for (int k = 0; k < count; ++k) {
+                const double dx = tile[0][k] - px;
+                const double dy = tile[1][k] - py;
+                const double dz = tile[2][k] - pz;
+                const double distSqr = __fma_rn(dz, dz, __fma_rn(dx, dx, dy * dy)) + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+                fx = __fma_rn(dx, invDist3, fx);
+                fy = __fma_rn(dy, invDist3, fy);
+                fz = __fma_rn(dz, invDist3, fz);
+            }
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        // Inactive threads in a partial block must also reach both barriers.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (active) {
+        const double vx = __dadd_rn(velocities[i], __dmul_rn(DT, fx));
+        const double vy = __dadd_rn(velocities[stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fy));
+        const double vz = __dadd_rn(velocities[2 * stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fz));
+        velocities[i] = vx;
+        velocities[stride + i] = vy;
+        velocities[2 * stride + i] = vz;
+        // A separate output buffer keeps every force calculation on the same
+        // position snapshot, even though integration is fused into this kernel.
+        nextPositions[i] = __fma_rn(vx, DT, px);
+        nextPositions[stride + i] = __fma_rn(vy, DT, py);
+        nextPositions[2 * stride + i] = __fma_rn(vz, DT, pz);
+    }
 }

+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, int steps)
+        : n_(bodies.size()), stride_((n_ + 31) / 32 * 32), state_(6 * stride_) {
+        // CUDA is required even for an empty simulation; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "initializing the CUDA runtime");
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            state_[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            state_[stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            state_[2 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            state_[3 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            state_[4 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            state_[5 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&storage_), 9 * stride_ * sizeof(double)),
+                  "allocating body state");
+        positions_ = storage_;
+        nextPositions_ = storage_ + 3 * stride_;
+        velocities_ = storage_ + 6 * stride_;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_, state_.data(), 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "uploading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, state_.data() + 3 * stride_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "uploading velocities");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "creating the simulation stream");
+
+        if (steps >= 4) {
+            // An even batch returns to the same position buffer, so this graph
+            // can be replayed without updating any device pointers or nodes.
+            graphSteps_ = steps < 32 ? steps / 2 * 2 : 32;
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream_, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                      "capturing the simulation graph");
+            for (int step = 0; step < graphSteps_; ++step) advance();
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "capturing simulation kernels");
+            cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream_, &graph), "finishing the simulation graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                      "instantiating the simulation graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "freeing the captured graph");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (graph_) checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(graph_), "freeing the simulation graph");
+        if (stream_) checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream_), "freeing the simulation stream");
+        if (storage_) checkCuda(cudaFree(storage_), "freeing body state");
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    void run(int steps) {
+        if (n_ == 0 || steps <= 0) return;
+        while (graph_ && positions_ == storage_ && steps >= graphSteps_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(graph_, stream_), "launching the simulation graph");
+            steps -= graphSteps_;
+        }
+        for (int step = 0; step < steps; ++step) advance();
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the simulation kernels");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_), "running the simulation");
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data(), positions_, 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data() + 3 * stride_, velocities_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "downloading velocities");
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(state_[i], state_[stride_ + i], state_[2 * stride_ + i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(state_[3 * stride_ + i], state_[4 * stride_ + i],
+                                 state_[5 * stride_ + i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    void advance() {
+        // One warp per block exposes many independent targets and avoids
+        // waiting for other warps at each shared-memory tile barrier.
+        constexpr int BlockSize = 32;
+        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>((n_ + BlockSize - 1) / BlockSize);
+        if (n_ < 4096) {
+            advanceBodies<BlockSize, 16><<<blocks, BlockSize, 0, stream_>>>(positions_, nextPositions_, velocities_, n_, stride_);
+        } else {
+            advanceBodies<BlockSize, 4><<<blocks, BlockSize, 0, stream_>>>(positions_, nextPositions_, velocities_, n_, stride_);
+        }
+        std::swap(positions_, nextPositions_);
     }
-}
 
+    const size_t n_, stride_;
+    std::vector<double> state_;
+    double* storage_ = nullptr;
+    double* positions_ = nullptr;
+    double* nextPositions_ = nullptr;
+    double* velocities_ = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t graph_ = nullptr;
+    int graphSteps_ = 0;
+};

    26	void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    27	    for (auto& body : bodies) {
    28	        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    29	        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;

   174	                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
   175	                  "downloading velocities");
   176	        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
   177	            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(state_[i], state_[stride_ + i], state_[2 * stride_ + i]);
   178	            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(state_[3 * stride_ + i], state_[4 * stride_ + i],
   179	                                 state_[5 * stride_ + i]);
   180	        }
   181	    }
   182	
   183	private:
   184	    void advance() {
   185	        // One warp per block exposes many independent targets and avoids
   186	        // waiting for other warps at each shared-memory tile barrier.
   187	        constexpr int BlockSize = 32;
   188	        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>((n_ + BlockSize - 1) / BlockSize);
   189	        if (n_ < 4096) {
   190	            advanceBodies<BlockSize, 16><<<blocks, BlockSize, 0, stream_>>>(positions_, nextPositions_, velocities_, n_, stride_);
   191	        } else {
   192	            advanceBodies<BlockSize, 4><<<blocks, BlockSize, 0, stream_>>>(positions_, nextPositions_, velocities_, n_, stride_);
   193	        }
   194	        std::swap(positions_, nextPositions_);
   195	    }
   196	
   197	    const size_t n_, stride_;
   198	    std::vector<double> state_;
   199	    double* storage_ = nullptr;
   200	    double* positions_ = nullptr;
   201	    double* nextPositions_ = nullptr;
   202	    double* velocities_ = nullptr;
   203	    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
   204	    cudaGraphExec_t graph_ = nullptr;
   205	    int graphSteps_ = 0;
   206	};
   207	
   208	double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
   209	    double energy = 0.0;
   210	    const size_t n = bodies.size();
   211	    
   212	    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
   213	    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
   214	        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
   215	                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
   216	                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
   217	    }
   218	    
   219	    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
   220	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   221	        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
   222	            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
   223	            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
   224	            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
   225	            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
   226	            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
   227	        }
   228	    }
   229	    
   230	    return energy;
   231	}
   232	
   233	// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
   234	bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
   235	    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
   310	    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
   311	    simulation.download(bodies);
   312	    
   313	    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
   314	    
   315	    // Print results for external validation
   316	    if (printResults) {
   317	        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
   318	        std::vector<double> bodyData;
   319	        bodyData.reserve(bodies.size() * 6);
   320	        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
   321	            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
   322	            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
   323	            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
   324	            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
   325	            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
   326	            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
   327	        }
   328	        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
   329	    }
   330	    
   331	    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
   332	    if (validate) {
   333	        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
   334	        
   335	        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
   336	            // Report final energy for reference
   337	            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
   338	            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
   339	            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   340	            return 0;
   341	        } else {
   342	            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   343	            return 1;
   344	        }
   345	    }
   346	    
   347	    return 0;
   348	}

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}

+// Each thread owns one target body. Source positions are loaded cooperatively
+// into shared memory and reused by all targets in the block.
+template <int BlockSize, int Unroll>
+__global__ void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positions,
+                              double* __restrict__ nextPositions,
+                              double* __restrict__ velocities,
+                              size_t n, size_t stride) {
+    __shared__ double tile[3][BlockSize];
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * BlockSize + lane;
+    const bool active = i < n;
+    const double px = active ? positions[i] : 0.0;
+    const double py = active ? positions[stride + i] : 0.0;
+    const double pz = active ? positions[2 * stride + i] : 0.0;
+    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < n; base += BlockSize) {
+        const size_t j = base + lane;
+        tile[0][lane] = j < n ? positions[j] : 0.0;
+        tile[1][lane] = j < n ? positions[stride + j] : 0.0;
+        tile[2][lane] = j < n ? positions[2 * stride + j] : 0.0;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int count = static_cast<int>(n - base < BlockSize ? n - base : BlockSize);
+        if (active) {
+            // Preserve increasing source-index order; no parallel force reduction
+            // or reduced-precision/approximate reciprocal square root is used.
+            #pragma unroll Unroll
+            for (int k = 0; k < count; ++k) {
+                const double dx = tile[0][k] - px;
+                const double dy = tile[1][k] - py;
+                const double dz = tile[2][k] - pz;
+                const double distSqr = __fma_rn(dz, dz, __fma_rn(dx, dx, dy * dy)) + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+                fx = __fma_rn(dx, invDist3, fx);
+                fy = __fma_rn(dy, invDist3, fy);
+                fz = __fma_rn(dz, invDist3, fz);
+            }
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        // Inactive threads in a partial block must also reach both barriers.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (active) {
+        const double vx = __dadd_rn(velocities[i], __dmul_rn(DT, fx));
+        const double vy = __dadd_rn(velocities[stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fy));
+        const double vz = __dadd_rn(velocities[2 * stride + i], __dmul_rn(DT, fz));
+        velocities[i] = vx;
+        velocities[stride + i] = vy;
+        velocities[2 * stride + i] = vz;
+        // A separate output buffer keeps every force calculation on the same
+        // position snapshot, even though integration is fused into this kernel.
+        nextPositions[i] = __fma_rn(vx, DT, px);
+        nextPositions[stride + i] = __fma_rn(vy, DT, py);
+        nextPositions[2 * stride + i] = __fma_rn(vz, DT, pz);
+    }
 }

+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, int steps)
+        : n_(bodies.size()), stride_((n_ + 31) / 32 * 32), state_(6 * stride_) {
+        // CUDA is required even for an empty simulation; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "initializing the CUDA runtime");
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            state_[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            state_[stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            state_[2 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            state_[3 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            state_[4 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            state_[5 * stride_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&storage_), 9 * stride_ * sizeof(double)),
+                  "allocating body state");
+        positions_ = storage_;
+        nextPositions_ = storage_ + 3 * stride_;
+        velocities_ = storage_ + 6 * stride_;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(positions_, state_.data(), 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "uploading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(velocities_, state_.data() + 3 * stride_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "uploading velocities");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                  "creating the simulation stream");
+
+        if (steps >= 4) {
+            // An even batch returns to the same position buffer, so this graph
+            // can be replayed without updating any device pointers or nodes.
+            graphSteps_ = steps < 32 ? steps / 2 * 2 : 32;
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream_, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                      "capturing the simulation graph");
+            for (int step = 0; step < graphSteps_; ++step) advance();
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "capturing simulation kernels");
+            cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream_, &graph), "finishing the simulation graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                      "instantiating the simulation graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "freeing the captured graph");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (graph_) checkCuda(cudaGraphExecDestroy(graph_), "freeing the simulation graph");
+        if (stream_) checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream_), "freeing the simulation stream");
+        if (storage_) checkCuda(cudaFree(storage_), "freeing body state");
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    void run(int steps) {
+        if (n_ == 0 || steps <= 0) return;
+        while (graph_ && positions_ == storage_ && steps >= graphSteps_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(graph_, stream_), "launching the simulation graph");
+            steps -= graphSteps_;
+        }
+        for (int step = 0; step < steps; ++step) advance();
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching the simulation kernels");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_), "running the simulation");
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
+        if (n_ == 0) return;
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data(), positions_, 3 * stride_ * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(state_.data() + 3 * stride_, velocities_,
+                             3 * stride_ * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "downloading velocities");
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(state_[i], state_[stride_ + i], state_[2 * stride_ + i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(state_[3 * stride_ + i], state_[4 * stride_ + i],
+                                 state_[5 * stride_ + i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    void advance() {
+        // One warp per block exposes many independent targets and avoids
+        // waiting for other warps at each shared-memory tile barrier.
+        constexpr int BlockSize = 32;
+        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>((n_ + BlockSize - 1) / BlockSize);
+        if (n_ < 4096) {
+            advanceBodies<BlockSize, 16><<<blocks, BlockSize, 0, stream_>>>(positions_, nextPositions_, velocities_, n_, stride_);
+        } else {
+            advanceBodies<BlockSize, 4><<<blocks, BlockSize, 0, stream_>>>(positions_, nextPositions_, velocities_, n_, stride_);
+        }
+        std::swap(positions_, nextPositions_);
     }
-}

+// Each thread owns one target body. Source positions are loaded cooperatively
+// into shared memory and reused by all targets in the block.
+template <int BlockSize, int Unroll>
+__global__ void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positions,
+                              double* __restrict__ nextPositions,
+                              double* __restrict__ velocities,
+                              size_t n, size_t stride) {
+    __shared__ double tile[3][BlockSize];
+    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * BlockSize + lane;
+    const bool active = i < n;
+    const double px = active ? positions[i] : 0.0;
+    const double py = active ? positions[stride + i] : 0.0;
+    const double pz = active ? positions[2 * stride + i] : 0.0;
